[Unit]
Description=SurrealDB persistence layer for Cohezion
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=mike-anderson
Group=mike-anderson
WorkingDirectory=/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion
EnvironmentFile=/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env
ExecStart=/home/mike-anderson/.surrealdb/surreal start --user ${SURREAL_USER} --pass ${SURREAL_PASS} file://${SURREAL_DATA_PATH}
Restart=always
RestartSec=5

[Install]

[Install]

{
  "skill": "spec",
  "args": "System Crash Root Cause Analysis and Prevention\n\n## Problem Statement\nThe Framework Desktop crashed and rebooted 3 times in rapid succession (17:35, 17:38, 17:41 on Feb 18). Investigation reveals a cascading failure originating from SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST corruption that has been crash-looping for 4+ days.\n\n## Evidence Gathered\n\n### Root Cause: SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST Corruption\n- `CURRENT` file points to `MANIFEST-190877` but only `MANIFEST-190909` exists on disk\n- Error: `IO error: No such file or directory: While opening a file for sequentially reading: MANIFEST-190877`\n- The corruption has been present since at least Feb 14 13:00 (originally looking for `MANIFEST-164765`)\n- Restart counter reached 129,220+ (every 5 seconds for 4+ days)\n- 57,476 files accumulated in the surrealdb data directory (mostly empty LOG.old files)\n\n### Cascading Service Failures\n- `cohezion-surreal.service` (system): crash-looping continuously (Restart=always, RestartSec=5)\n- `cohezion-lab.service` (user): failing with \"Failed to determine supplementary groups: Operation not permitted\" (User= field in user service)\n- `ngrok-tunnel.service` (user): \"Start request repeated too quickly\" - crash-looping\n- `xdg-desktop-portal-gnome`: core dump (SIGABRT) - likely the actual desktop crash trigger\n- `systemd-journald`: crash report exists from Feb 17\n\n### Historical Crash Reports in /var/crash/\n- `_usr_share_antigravity_antigravity.1000.crash` (Feb 11)\n- `_usr_lib_systemd_systemd-journald.0.crash` (Feb 17) \n- `_usr_libexec_xdg-desktop-portal-gnome.1000.crash` (Feb 18)\n- `_usr_libexec_gnome-terminal-server.1000.crash` (Feb 14)\n\n### System State After Recovery (Current Boot)\n- Memory: 125Gi total, 14Gi used, 110Gi available - NOT memory pressure\n- Swap: 40Gi, 0B used - NOT swap thrashing\n- Disk: 7% used (1.3TB avail) - NOT disk pressure\n- Load: 1.35, 0.96, 0.63 - reasonable\n- Ollama: running fine\n- No OOM kills or kernel panics detected in kernel logs\n- Boot -3 uptime was 8+ days (Feb 10 to Feb 18)\n\n### Service Configuration Issues Found\n1. `cohezion-surreal.service`: `Restart=always` with `RestartSec=5` and NO restart limits (StartLimitBurst/StartLimitIntervalSec) - enables infinite crash loops\n2. `cohezion-lab.service`: Has `User=mike-anderson` in a user-scoped service (invalid - user services already run as the user, this causes supplementary group errors)\n3. `ngrok-tunnel.service`: Also in crash loop due to upstream dependency failures\n4. SurrealDB uses deprecated `file://` protocol (should use `surrealkv://` or `rocksdb://`)\n5. SurrealDB block cache set to 66GB (!) on 128GB system - dangerously high for a dev workstation running many services\n\n## Scope\nThis needs to:\n1. Fix the immediate SurrealDB MANIFEST corruption\n2. Clean up 57K accumulated log files\n3. Add crash-loop prevention to ALL Cohezion systemd services\n4. Fix the cohezion-lab.service User= misconfiguration\n5. Create a universal service health watchdog/guardian that works regardless of which AI coding tool (Claude Code, Antigravity, Gemini CLI, OpenCode) is running\n6. Establish resource limits and safeguards for all services\n7. Verify all systems are back online"
}

> AGENT

**Step 1.2: Task Understanding**

My understanding of this task:

**Core problem:** A SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST corruption caused a 4-day crash loop (129K+ restarts) that eventually cascaded into a full desktop crash with 3 rapid reboots. The fundamental issue is that Cohezion's systemd services have no crash-loop protection, no resource limits, and no health monitoring - making the system vulnerable to any single service failure snowballing into a machine-level crash.

**What needs to happen:**
1. **Immediate triage** - Fix the corrupted SurrealDB data, clean up 57K accumulated files, stop the crash loops
2. **Harden services** - Add systemd restart limits, resource caps, and proper dependency chains to ALL Cohezion services
3. **Watchdog** - Create a lightweight health monitor that detects crash loops early and alerts/stops them, working independently of any AI coding tool

**Assumptions:**
- SurrealDB data is expendable (dev environment) - we can rebuild from a fresh DB if repair fails
- The `file://` protocol deprecation in SurrealDB should be addressed while we're in there
- The 66GB block cache config is auto-calculated by SurrealDB, not user-configured (we should cap it)

The task is clear and well-scoped from the evidence. Let me explore the service configurations and existing infrastructure.

    14→    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
    15→)

    20→    logger.info("🛡️ Cohezion Service Watchdog Started.")
    21→    
    22→    # Target components
    23→    targets = ["surrealdb", "api", "simulation", "recorder"]
    24→    
    25→    try:
    26→        while True:
    27→            for component in targets:
    28→                if not dm.is_running(dm.COMPONENTS[component]["cmd_signature"]):
    29→                    logger.warning(f"⚠️ {component.upper()} is down! Restarting...")
    30→                    dm.start_component(component)
    31→                else:
    32→                    # Optional: Port check for deeper health validation
    33→                    pass
    34→            
    35→            # Check every 30 seconds - low overhead
    36→            time.sleep(30)
    37→            
    38→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
    39→        logger.info("Watchdog stopped by user.")
    40→    except Exception as e:
    41→        logger.error(f"Watchdog crashed: {e}")
    42→
    43→if __name__ == "__main__":
    44→    main()
    45→

     1→import time
     2→import subprocess
     3→import logging
     4→from pathlib import Path
     5→
     6→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s")
     7→logger = logging.getLogger("HealthMonitor")
     8→
     9→REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent.resolve()
    10→JANITOR_SCRIPT = REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "repo_janitor.py"
    11→CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 3600  # Hourly
    12→
    13→from cohezion.healing import get_healing_system, HealthStatus
    14→
    15→def run_janitor():
    16→    logger.info("🕒 Running scheduled repository health check...")
    17→    try:
    18→        # Check vitals first
    19→        import sys
    20→        sys.path.append(str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts"))
    21→        import repo_janitor
    22→        vitals = repo_janitor.check_git_vitals(use_cache=False)
    23→
    24→        # Register with healing system
    25→        healing = get_healing_system()
    26→        status = healing.detector.check(
    27→            "repository", "bloat", vitals.get("pending_count", 0), 1000
    28→        )
    29→        
    30→        if status.status != "healthy":
    31→            logger.warning(f"⚠️ Repository degradation detected: {status.status}. Triggering autonomous cleanup.")
    32→            subprocess.run(["python3", str(JANITOR_SCRIPT)], cwd=REPO_ROOT, check=True)
    33→            
    34→            # Database pruning
    35→            logger.info("🧹 Performing autonomous database pruning...")
    36→            subprocess.run(["python3", str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "db_pruning.py"), "7"], cwd=REPO_ROOT, check=True)
    37→
    38→            # Security Audit
    39→            logger.info("🛡️ Performing autonomous security audit...")
    40→            subprocess.run(["python3", str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "security_scout.py")], cwd=REPO_ROOT, check=True)
    41→
    42→            # Auto-checkpoint if on a task branch
    43→            branch = subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    44→            if branch != "main" and branch != "master":
    45→                logger.info(f"Autonomous checkpointing for branch: {branch}")
    46→                subprocess.run(["python3", str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "work_manager.py"), "checkpoint", "-m", "Autonomic hygiene checkpoint"], cwd=REPO_ROOT)
    47→
    48→        logger.info("✅ Health check complete.")
    49→    except Exception as e:
    50→        logger.error(f"❌ Autonomic action failed: {e}")

    31→- **Feb 14 18:35**: Briefly gets "Address already in use" errors (overlapping restarts), then back to MANIFEST errors.

    33→- **Feb 17**: systemd-journald crashes (crash report in `/var/crash/`). Likely from journal bloat processing 129K+ service restart log entries.
    34→- **Feb 18 ~17:35**: Desktop crashes. `xdg-desktop-portal-gnome` core dumps (SIGABRT). Three rapid reboots follow (17:35, 17:38, 17:41). The crash loop resumes immediately after each reboot.
    35→
    36→### Cascade Chain
    37→```
    38→RocksDB MANIFEST corruption
    39→  → SurrealDB fails to start (every 5s, Restart=always, no limits)
    40→  → 129K+ process spawns over 4 days
    41→  → journald overwhelmed processing restart logs → journald crash (Feb 17)
    42→  → cohezion-lab also crash-looping (User= bug in user service, 60s interval)
    43→  → Combined: process churn + journal corruption → desktop portal crash → system reboot
    44→  → Reboots can't help: same corruption persists → immediate re-crash → 3 rapid reboots
    45→```
    46→
    47→### Why It Wasn't Caught
    48→1. **No restart limits**: `cohezion-surreal.service` has `Restart=always` + `RestartSec=5` with NO `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`. systemd will restart it forever.
    49→2. **No monitoring**: The existing `service_watchdog.py` and `health_monitor.py` scripts depend on `DaemonManager` which doesn't exist — they've never worked.
    50→3. **No resource limits**: Services have no `MemoryMax`, `CPUQuota`, or `TasksMax` constraints.
    51→
    52→## Scope
    53→
    54→### In Scope
    55→- Fix SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST pointer corruption
    56→- Clean up 57K accumulated LOG files in surrealdb data directory
    57→- Harden ALL Cohezion systemd services with restart limits and resource caps
    58→- Fix cohezion-lab.service misconfiguration (User= in user service, WantedBy=)
    59→- Remove broken symlinks for deleted services (antiquarian, mycelium, shadow)
    60→- Create a lightweight systemd-native service guardian (no Python dependencies)
    61→- Update SurrealDB from deprecated `file://` to `rocksdb://` protocol
    62→- Bring all services back online and verify
    63→
    64→### Out of Scope
    65→- Rewriting `DaemonManager` / `wake_up.py` / `service_watchdog.py` (dead code, needs separate cleanup)
    66→- SurrealDB upgrade (staying on 2.4.1)
    67→- Changes to the Antigravity crash (separate issue, different crash report)
    68→- GNOME desktop portal stability (upstream bug, not our code)
    69→- Modifying application code (this is infrastructure-only)
    70→
    71→## Prerequisites
    72→- sudo access for system-level service file edits (`cohezion-surreal.service` is in `/etc/systemd/system/`)
    73→- SurrealDB binary at `/home/mike-anderson/.surrealdb/surreal` (confirmed present, v2.4.1)
    74→
    75→## Context for Implementer
    76→
    77→- **Service file locations:**
    78→  - System service: `/etc/systemd/system/cohezion-surreal.service`
    79→  - User services: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-lab.service`, `ngrok-tunnel.service`, `ngrok-watchdog.service`

    83→- **Env file:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env` contains `SURREAL_DATA_PATH` and credentials. NOTE: `SURREAL_PASS` uses a Python one-liner that regenerates on each read — this is a separate bug but not in scope.
    84→- **Pattern to follow:** The `ngrok-tunnel.service` already has proper restart limits (`StartLimitBurst=5`, `StartLimitInterval=600`). Use this as the template for other services.

   158→### Task 3: Harden cohezion-surreal.service
   159→
   160→**Objective:** Add crash-loop prevention, resource limits, and update the SurrealDB protocol from deprecated `file://` to `rocksdb://`.
   161→
   162→**Dependencies:** Task 1

   170→- Change `file://${SURREAL_DATA_PATH}` to `rocksdb://${SURREAL_DATA_PATH}` (per SurrealDB deprecation warning)
   171→- Add resource limits: `MemoryMax=16G` (cap at 16GB, prevents the 66GB block cache from consuming all RAM), `CPUQuota=200%` (limit to 2 cores), `TasksMax=256`
   172→- Add `ExecStartPre` health check that verifies MANIFEST file exists before starting
   173→- Requires `sudo` for editing system service files
   174→- Run `sudo systemctl daemon-reload` after changes

   272→### Task 7: Create systemd Service Guardian
   273→
   274→**Objective:** Create a lightweight, dependency-free systemd timer + script that monitors all Cohezion services for crash loops and stops runaway services before they cascade into system failures. Must work regardless of which AI coding tool is active.
   275→
   276→**Dependencies:** Task 3, Task 4, Task 6 (services must be hardened first)
   277→

   279→- Create: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh`

   281→- Create: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-guardian.timer`
   282→
   283→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   284→- Pure bash script (no Python dependencies, no cohezion imports — survives any codebase breakage)
   285→- Runs every 5 minutes via systemd timer
   286→- Checks all cohezion-* services for:
   287→  - Rapid restart count (>3 restarts in 10 minutes → stop the service, log alert)
   288→  - "activating (auto-restart)" state persisting for >5 minutes → stop the service
   289→  - Disk usage in data/surrealdb/ exceeding threshold (>10K files or >1GB)
   290→- Actions: stop runaway service, write alert to syslog, optionally send desktop notification
   291→- Script is idempotent (safe to run multiple times)
   292→- Does NOT restart services (that's the user's job after investigation)
   293→- The timer itself has restart limits to prevent meta-crash-loops
   294→

   298→- [ ] Timer fires every 5 minutes

   300→- [ ] Guardian logs actions to journal

   338→
   339→- **Task 1-2:** Manual verification (data repair, file cleanup)
   340→- **Task 3-6:** `systemctl show` to verify service properties match expected values

   343→
   344→## Risks and Mitigations
   345→
   346→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   347→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   348→| MANIFEST-190909 is also corrupt | Low | High | Backup data dir before repair. If SurrealDB still fails after fix, reinitialize with fresh DB (dev data is expendable) |
   349→| rocksdb:// protocol behaves differently from file:// | Low | Medium | Test SurrealDB startup immediately after protocol change. Revert if issues |
   350→| Service guardian false-positives | Medium | Low | Guardian only STOPS services, never restarts. False-positive = service stopped, user investigates. Safe default |
   351→| Hardened restart limits too strict | Low | Low | 5 restarts per 10 minutes is generous. Can be tuned later based on operational experience |
   352→| SURREAL_PASS regenerates on each restart | Medium | Medium | Out of scope for this plan (noted in Context). Needs separate fix to use a static secret |

   273→
   274→**Objective:** Create a lightweight, dependency-free systemd timer + script that monitors all Cohezion services for crash loops and stops runaway services before they cascade into system failures. Must work regardless of which AI coding tool is active.

     1→# System Crash Root Cause Analysis and Prevention Plan
     2→
     3→Created: 2026-02-18
     4→Status: PENDING
     5→Approved: No
     6→Iterations: 0
     7→Worktree: No

    18→
    19→## Summary
    20→
    21→**Goal:** Fix SurrealDB RocksDB corruption that caused a 4-day crash loop (129K+ restarts), harden all Cohezion systemd services with crash-loop prevention and resource limits, and create a lightweight service guardian that protects the system regardless of which AI tool (Claude Code, Antigravity, Gemini CLI, OpenCode) is running.
    22→
    23→**Architecture:** Three-layer defense: (1) Fix immediate corruption and clean up, (2) Harden all systemd services with restart limits, resource caps, and proper configuration, (3) Deploy a systemd-native watchdog timer that detects crash-looping services and stops them before they cascade into system-level failures.
    24→
    25→**Tech Stack:** systemd (service hardening, timers), bash (guardian script), SurrealDB 2.4.1 (RocksDB repair)
    26→
    27→## Root Cause Analysis
    28→
    29→### Timeline
    30→- **Feb 14 ~13:00**: SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST corruption first appears. `CURRENT` file points to `MANIFEST-164765` which no longer exists on disk. SurrealDB begins crash-looping every 5 seconds.
    31→- **Feb 14 18:35**: Briefly gets "Address already in use" errors (overlapping restarts), then back to MANIFEST errors.
    32→- **Feb 14-18**: 129,220+ restart cycles accumulate. 57,476 LOG files created in surrealdb data directory (399MB). Each restart: fork process → init RocksDB (allocate 66GB block cache intent) → hit MANIFEST error → exit → repeat every 5 seconds.
    33→- **Feb 17**: systemd-journald crashes (crash report in `/var/crash/`). Likely from journal bloat processing 129K+ service restart log entries.
    34→- **Feb 18 ~17:35**: Desktop crashes. `xdg-desktop-portal-gnome` core dumps (SIGABRT). Three rapid reboots follow (17:35, 17:38, 17:41). The crash loop resumes immediately after each reboot.
    35→
    36→### Cascade Chain
    37→```
    38→RocksDB MANIFEST corruption
    39→  → SurrealDB fails to start (every 5s, Restart=always, no limits)
    40→  → 129K+ process spawns over 4 days
    41→  → journald overwhelmed processing restart logs → journald crash (Feb 17)
    42→  → cohezion-lab also crash-looping (User= bug in user service, 60s interval)
    43→  → Combined: process churn + journal corruption → desktop portal crash → system reboot
    44→  → Reboots can't help: same corruption persists → immediate re-crash → 3 rapid reboots
    45→```
    46→
    47→### Why It Wasn't Caught
    48→1. **No restart limits**: `cohezion-surreal.service` has `Restart=always` + `RestartSec=5` with NO `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`. systemd will restart it forever.
    49→2. **No monitoring**: The existing `service_watchdog.py` and `health_monitor.py` scripts depend on `DaemonManager` which doesn't exist — they've never worked.
    50→3. **No resource limits**: Services have no `MemoryMax`, `CPUQuota`, or `TasksMax` constraints.
    51→
    52→## Scope
    53→
    54→### In Scope
    55→- Fix SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST pointer corruption
    56→- Clean up 57K accumulated LOG files in surrealdb data directory
    57→- Harden ALL Cohezion systemd services with restart limits and resource caps
    58→- Fix cohezion-lab.service misconfiguration (User= in user service, WantedBy=)
    59→- Remove broken symlinks for deleted services (antiquarian, mycelium, shadow)
    60→- Create a lightweight systemd-native service guardian (no Python dependencies)
    61→- Update SurrealDB from deprecated `file://` to `rocksdb://` protocol
    62→- Bring all services back online and verify
    63→
    64→### Out of Scope
    65→- Rewriting `DaemonManager` / `wake_up.py` / `service_watchdog.py` (dead code, needs separate cleanup)
    66→- SurrealDB upgrade (staying on 2.4.1)
    67→- Changes to the Antigravity crash (separate issue, different crash report)
    68→- GNOME desktop portal stability (upstream bug, not our code)
    69→- Modifying application code (this is infrastructure-only)
    70→
    71→## Prerequisites
    72→- sudo access for system-level service file edits (`cohezion-surreal.service` is in `/etc/systemd/system/`)
    73→- SurrealDB binary at `/home/mike-anderson/.surrealdb/surreal` (confirmed present, v2.4.1)

SurrealDB begins crash-looping every 5 seconds.\n- **Feb 14 18:35**: Briefly gets \"Address already in use\" errors (overlapping restarts), then back to MANIFEST errors.\n- **Feb 14-18**: 129,220+ restart cycles accumulate. 57,476 LOG files created in surrealdb data directory (399MB). Each restart: fork process → init RocksDB → hit MANIFEST error → exit → repeat every 5 seconds.\n- **Feb 17**: systemd-journald crashes (crash report in `/var/crash/`). Journal has grown to 1.2GB from processing 129K+ service restart log entries.\n- **Feb 18 ~17:35**: Desktop crashes. `xdg-desktop-portal-gnome` core dumps (SIGABRT). Three rapid reboots follow (17:35, 17:38, 17:41). The crash loop resumes immediately after each reboot.\n\n### Cascade Chain\n```\nRocksDB MANIFEST corruption\n  → SurrealDB fails to start (every 5s, Restart=always, no limits)\n  → 129K+ process spawns over 4 days (each initializing RocksDB - heavy I/O + memory allocation)\n  → journald overwhelmed processing restart logs (1.2GB journal) → journald crash (Feb 17)\n  → cohezion-lab also generating journal noise (User= bug, status 216/GROUP every 60s)\n    NOTE: cohezion-lab exit 216 is lightweight (systemd rejects before forking) - contributes\n    journal noise but NOT process churn. SurrealDB is the primary resource drain.\n  → Combined: process churn + journal bloat → desktop portal crash → system reboot\n  → Reboots can't help: same corruption persists → immediate re-crash → 3 rapid reboots\n```\n\n### Why It Wasn't Caught\n1. **No restart limits**: `cohezion-surreal.service` has `Restart=always` + `RestartSec=5` with NO `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`. systemd will restart it forever.\n2. **No monitoring**: The existing `service_watchdog.py` and `health_monitor.py` scripts depend on `DaemonManager` which doesn't exist — they've never worked.\n3. **No resource limits**: Services have no `MemoryMax`, `CPUQuota`, or `TasksMax` constraints.\n\n## Scope\n\n### In Scope\n- Fix SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST pointer corruption\n- Clean up 57K accumulated LOG The PRIMARY prevention is `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec` in each service's `[Unit]` section. The guardian catches edge cases: services without proper limits, slow-burn crash loops under burst thresholds, or manual restarts of failed services.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 3, Task 4, Task 6 (services must be hardened first)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh`\n- Create: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-guardian.service`\n- Create: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-guardian.timer`\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Pure bash script (no Python dependencies, no cohezion imports — survives any codebase breakage)\n- Runs every 2 minutes via systemd timer (reduced from 5 for faster detection)\n- Checks all cohezion-* and ngrok-* services for:\n  - High restart count via `NRestarts` property (>10 since boot → alert)\n  - \"activating (auto-restart)\" state with \"Result: exit-code\" → stop if user service, alert if system service\n  - Disk usage in data/surrealdb/ exceeding threshold (>10K files or >1GB)\n- **Privilege boundary:** Guardian runs as user service. For user services (cohezion-lab, ngrok-*), it can stop them directly. For system services (cohezion-surreal), it can only LOG alerts and send desktop notifications (cannot `systemctl stop` without sudo). This is documented and acceptable — StartLimitBurst is the primary prevention for system services.\n- Add timeout to prevent guardian from hanging: `ExecStart=/usr/bin/timeout 60 /path/to/service_guardian.sh` and `TimeoutStartSec=90`\n- Actions: stop runaway user services, log alerts for system services, write to syslog, send desktop notification via `notify-send`\n- Script is idempotent (safe to run multiple times)\n- Does NOT restart services (that's the user's job after investigation)\n- The timer itself has restart limits to prevent meta-crash-loops\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] Guardian script exists and is executable\n- [ ] Guardian has timeout wrapper (60s)\n- [ ] Timer fires every 2 minutes\n- [ ] Guardian has no Python dependencies (pure bash + standard utils)\n- [ ] Guardian logs actions to journal\n- [ ] Guardian correctly handles user vs system service privilege boundary\n\n**Verify:**\n- `bash -n /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` — syntax check passes\n- `bash /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` — runs without errors on healthy system\n- `systemctl --user is-active cohezion-guardian.timer` shows `active`\n\n### Task 8: Bring Services Online and Verify\n\n**Objective:** Start all services, verify SurrealDB is healthy, confirm no crash loops, and run a smoke test.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 3, Task 4, Task 5, Task 6, Task 7\n\n**Files:**\n- No new files\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Start order: cohezion-surreal → verify SurrealDB responds → start ngrok → start guardian timer\n- cohezion-lab stays disabled (no entry point)\n- Verify SurrealDB: attempt connection with `surreal sql` or health endpoint\n- **SURREAL_PASS**: Now a static token in .env. Source .env to get the password for client connections.\n- Watch journal for 60 seconds to confirm no crash-loop restarts\n- Verify guardian timer is running and has fired at least once\n- **Protocol verification**: If rocksdb:// was configured, verify SurrealDB started without errors. If it fails, revert to file:// as documented in Task 3.\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] cohezion-surreal.service is active and running (not masked)\n- [ ] SurrealDB responds to queries\n- [ ] ngrok-tunnel.service is active (or stopped cleanly if no tunnel needed)\n- [ ] cohezion-guardian.timer is active and waiting\n- [ ] No services in crash loop (journal clean for 60 seconds)\n- [ ] cohezion-lab.service is disabled (documented: needs lab_driver.py)\n\n**Verify:**\n- `systemctl is-active cohezion-surreal.service` shows `active`\n- `journalctl -u cohezion-surreal.service --since \"1 minute ago\" --no-pager` shows healthy startup, no errors\n- `systemctl --user is-active cohezion-guardian.timer` shows `active`\n- `systemctl --user list-units cohezion* ngrok* --all --no-pager` shows no \"failed\" or \"activating\" units\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n- **Task 1:** Manual SurrealDB start test validates DB recovery. If DB is unrecoverable, fresh init.\n- **Task 2:** Manual verification (file cleanup, journal vacuum)\n- **Task 3-6:** `systemctl show` to verify service properties match expected values. Confirm StartLimit in [Unit] section via `systemctl cat`.\n- **Task 7:** Syntax check (`bash -n`), dry-run execution, verify timer activation\n- **Task 8:** End-to-end: start services → watch journal → verify health → confirm guardian detects nothing wrong\n\n## Risks and Mitigations\n\n| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|-----------|--------|------------|\n| MANIFEST-190909 is also corrupt or references missing SST files | Medium | High | Validate DB immediately in Task 1 (not Task 8). If validation fails, reinitialize with fresh DB — dev data is expendable |\n| rocksdb:// protocol behaves differently from file:// | Low | Medium | Test separately: first verify file:// works, then switch to rocksdb://. Two verification points, not one combined change |\n| MemoryMax=16G triggers OOM-kill of SurrealDB (66GB block cache) | Medium | Low | OOM-kill + StartLimitBurst=5 = at most 5 OOM kills in 10 min, then stop. Far better than infinite crash loop. SurrealDB 2.4.1 doesn't expose RocksDB block_cache_size tuning. Monitor empirically after startup |\n| Service guardian false-positives | Medium | Low | Guardian only STOPS user services, never restarts. For system services, alert only. False-positive = service stopped, user investigates. Safe default |\n| Guardian script hangs (systemd overloaded) | Low | Low | ExecStart wraps script with `/usr/bin/timeout 60`. TimeoutStartSec=90 in service unit. Prevents accumulation of blocked instances |\n| Accidental service re-enable during implementation | Low | Medium | Services masked in Task 1, unmasked only after hardening in Task 3/4. Prevents reboot from restarting unhardened services |\n\n## Open Questions\n\n- None — all questions resolved during investigation and plan verification\n\n### Deferred Ideas\n\n- Rewrite `DaemonManager` and `wake_up.py` to use systemd commands instead of custom process management\n- Research if SurrealDB has a config file or env var to set RocksDB block_cache_size explicitly (would eliminate the MemoryMax OOM risk)\n- Add Prometheus/Grafana monitoring for long-term observability\n- Create a `/wake` skill that integrates with the guardian for AI-tool-aware service management\n- Move cohezion-surreal.service from system scope to user scope (it already runs as mike-anderson, no need for system service)\n- Add polkit rule to allow user to stop system services without sudo (alternative to scope migration)\n"

    19→## Summary
    20→
    21→**Goal:** Fix SurrealDB RocksDB corruption that caused a 4-day crash loop (129K+ restarts), harden all Cohezion systemd services with crash-loop prevention and resource limits, and create a lightweight service guardian as a secondary safety net that protects the system regardless of which AI tool (Claude Code, Antigravity, Gemini CLI, OpenCode) is running.
    22→
    23→**Architecture:** Three-layer defense: (1) Fix immediate corruption and clean up, (2) Harden all systemd services with restart limits (PRIMARY crash-loop prevention via `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec` in `[Unit]` section), resource caps, and proper configuration, (3) Deploy a systemd-native watchdog timer as a SECONDARY safety net that catches cases where services lack proper limits, or where slow-burn crash loops stay under burst thresholds.
    24→
    25→**Tech Stack:** systemd (service hardening, timers), bash (guardian script), SurrealDB 2.4.1 (RocksDB repair)
    26→
    27→## Root Cause Analysis
    28→
    29→### Timeline
    30→- **Feb 14 ~13:00**: SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST corruption first appears. `CURRENT` file points to `MANIFEST-164765` which no longer exists on disk. SurrealDB begins crash-looping every 5 seconds.
    31→- **Feb 14 18:35**: Briefly gets "Address already in use" errors (overlapping restarts), then back to MANIFEST errors.
    32→- **Feb 14-18**: 129,220+ restart cycles accumulate. 57,476 LOG files created in surrealdb data directory (399MB). Each restart: fork process → init RocksDB → hit MANIFEST error → exit → repeat every 5 seconds.
    33→- **Feb 17**: systemd-journald crashes (crash report in `/var/crash/`). Journal has grown to 1.2GB from processing 129K+ service restart log entries.
    34→- **Feb 18 ~17:35**: Desktop crashes. `xdg-desktop-portal-gnome` core dumps (SIGABRT). Three rapid reboots follow (17:35, 17:38, 17:41). The crash loop resumes immediately after each reboot.
    35→
    36→### Cascade Chain
    37→```
    38→RocksDB MANIFEST corruption
    39→  → SurrealDB fails to start (every 5s, Restart=always, no limits)
    40→  → 129K+ process spawns over 4 days (each initializing RocksDB - heavy I/O + memory allocation)
    41→  → journald overwhelmed processing restart logs (1.2GB journal) → journald crash (Feb 17)
    42→  → cohezion-lab also generating journal noise (User= bug, status 216/GROUP every 60s)
    43→    NOTE: cohezion-lab exit 216 is lightweight (systemd rejects before forking) - contributes
    44→    journal noise but NOT process churn. SurrealDB is the primary resource drain.
    45→  → Combined: process churn + journal bloat → desktop portal crash → system reboot
    46→  → Reboots can't help: same corruption persists → immediate re-crash → 3 rapid reboots
    47→```
    48→
    49→### Why It Wasn't Caught
    50→1. **No restart limits**: `cohezion-surreal.service` has `Restart=always` + `RestartSec=5` with NO `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`. systemd will restart it forever.
    51→2. **No monitoring**: The existing `service_watchdog.py` and `health_monitor.py` scripts depend on `DaemonManager` which doesn't exist — they've never worked.
    52→3. **No resource limits**: Services have no `MemoryMax`, `CPUQuota`, or `TasksMax` constraints.
    53→
    54→## Scope
    55→

    57→- Fix SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST pointer corruption
    58→- Clean up 57K accumulated LOG files in surrealdb data directory + vacuum journal
    59→- Harden ALL Cohezion systemd services with restart limits and resource caps
    60→- Fix cohezion-lab.service misconfiguration (User= in user service, WantedBy=)
    61→- Remove broken symlinks for deleted services (antiquarian, mycelium, shadow)
    62→- Create a lightweight systemd-native service guardian (no Python dependencies)
    63→- Update SurrealDB from deprecated `file://` to `rocksdb://` protocol (tested separately from MANIFEST fix)
    64→- Generate a static SURREAL_PASS to replace the broken shell-substitution in .env
    65→- Bring all services back online and verify
    66→
    67→### Out of Scope
    68→- Rewriting `DaemonManager` / `wake_up.py` / `service_watchdog.py` (dead code, needs separate cleanup)
    69→- SurrealDB upgrade (staying on 2.4.1)
    70→- Changes to the Antigravity crash (separate issue, different crash report)
    71→- GNOME desktop portal stability (upstream bug, not our code)
    72→- Modifying application code (this is infrastructure-only)
    73→

    75→- sudo access for system-level service file edits (`cohezion-surreal.service` is in `/etc/systemd/system/`)
    76→- SurrealDB binary at `/home/mike-anderson/.surrealdb/surreal` (confirmed present, v2.4.1)

    78→## Context for Implementer
    79→
    80→- **Service file locations:**
    81→  - System service: `/etc/systemd/system/cohezion-surreal.service`
    82→  - User services: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-lab.service`, `ngrok-tunnel.service`, `ngrok-watchdog.service`
    83→  - Project templates: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/systemd/` (cohezion-lab.service, cohezion-simulation.service/timer)
    84→  - Broken symlinks: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-{antiquarian,mycelium,shadow}.service` → deleted targets
    85→- **SurrealDB data:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/data/surrealdb/` (399MB, 57K files, `CURRENT` points to `MANIFEST-190877`, only `MANIFEST-190909` exists, 1 SST file, 1 WAL file)
    86→- **Env file:** `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.env` contains `SURREAL_DATA_PATH` and credentials.
    87→- **CRITICAL - SURREAL_PASS:** The `.env` file has `SURREAL_PASS=$(python3 -c "import secrets; print(secrets.token_urlsafe(24))")`. systemd's `EnvironmentFile=` does NOT evaluate shell commands — it reads the literal string `$(python3 ...)` as the password. This means SurrealDB has been using a literal garbage string as its password (not regenerating). Shell clients that `source .env` get a different (evaluated) password and cannot authenticate. This must be fixed with a static password.
    88→- **Pattern to follow:** The `ngrok-tunnel.service` already has proper restart limits (`StartLimitBurst=5`, `StartLimitInterval=600`). Use this as the template for other services.
    89→- **CRITICAL - StartLimit placement:** `StartLimitBurst` and `StartLimitIntervalSec` MUST go in the `[Unit]` section, not `[Service]`. In `[Service]`, they may be silently ignored on systemd 255 (confirmed running). This applies to ALL service files.
    90→- **Gotcha:** cohezion-lab.service has `User=mike-anderson` but it's a user-scoped service (runs under `systemctl --user`). User services already run as the invoking user — specifying `User=` causes "Failed to determine supplementary groups: Operation not permitted". Remove it.
    91→- **Gotcha:** cohezion-lab.service depends on `surrealdb.service` but the actual service is `cohezion-surreal.service`. This dependency reference is wrong.

   198→- **Restart limits in [Unit] section** (NOT [Service] — critical for systemd 255):

The `MemoryMax=16G` limit will cause SurrealDB to be OOM-killed if it tries to allocate 66GB. However, with `StartLimitBurst=5`, this converts to at most 5 OOM-kills in 10 minutes before systemd gives up — far better than infinite crash loops. If SurrealDB's RocksDB respects cgroup limits for mmap allocations, the block cache may auto-scale down. Test empirically.

   317→
   318→### Task 7: Create systemd Service Guardian
   319→
   320→**Objective:** Create a lightweight, dependency-free systemd timer + script as a SECONDARY safety net that monitors all Cohezion services for crash loops. The PRIMARY prevention is `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec` in each service's `[Unit]` section. The guardian catches edge cases: services without proper limits, slow-burn crash loops under burst thresholds, or manual restarts of failed services.
   321→
   322→**Dependencies:** Task 3, Task 4, Task 6 (services must be hardened first)
   323→
   324→**Files:**
   325→- Create: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh`
   326→- Create: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-guardian.service`
   327→- Create: `~/.config/systemd/user/cohezion-guardian.timer`
   328→
   329→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   330→- Pure bash script (no Python dependencies, no cohezion imports — survives any codebase breakage)
   331→- Runs every 2 minutes via systemd timer (reduced from 5 for faster detection)
   332→- Checks all cohezion-* and ngrok-* services for:
   333→  - High restart count via `NRestarts` property (>10 since boot → alert)
   334→  - "activating (auto-restart)" state with "Result: exit-code" → stop if user service, alert if system service
   335→  - Disk usage in data/surrealdb/ exceeding threshold (>10K files or >1GB)
   336→- **Privilege boundary:** Guardian runs as user service. For user services (cohezion-lab, ngrok-*), it can stop them directly. For system services (cohezion-surreal), it can only LOG alerts and send desktop notifications (cannot `systemctl stop` without sudo). This is documented and acceptable — StartLimitBurst is the primary prevention for system services.
   337→- Add timeout to prevent guardian from hanging: `ExecStart=/usr/bin/timeout 60 /path/to/service_guardian.sh` and `TimeoutStartSec=90`
   338→- Actions: stop runaway user services, log alerts for system services, write to syslog, send desktop notification via `notify-send`
   339→- Script is idempotent (safe to run multiple times)
   340→- Does NOT restart services (that's the user's job after investigation)
   341→- The timer itself has restart limits to prevent meta-crash-loops
   342→
   343→**Definition of Done:**
   344→- [ ] Guardian script exists and is executable
   345→- [ ] Guardian has timeout wrapper (60s)
   346→- [ ] Timer fires every 2 minutes
   347→- [ ] Guardian has no Python dependencies (pure bash + standard utils)
   348→- [ ] Guardian logs actions to journal
   349→- [ ] Guardian correctly handles user vs system service privilege boundary
   350→
   351→**Verify:**
   352→- `bash -n /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` — syntax check passes
   353→- `bash /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` — runs without errors on healthy system
   354→- `systemctl --user is-active cohezion-guardian.timer` shows `active`
   355→
   356→### Task 8: Bring Services Online and Verify
   357→
   358→**Objective:** Start all services, verify SurrealDB is healthy, confirm no crash loops, and run a smoke test.
   359→
   360→**Dependencies:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 3, Task 4, Task 5, Task 6, Task 7
   361→
   362→**Files:**
   363→- No new files
   364→
   365→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   366→- Start order: cohezion-surreal → verify SurrealDB responds → start ngrok → start guardian timer
   367→- cohezion-lab stays disabled (no entry point)
   368→- Verify SurrealDB: attempt connection with `surreal sql` or health endpoint
   369→- **SURREAL_PASS**: Now a static token in .env. Source .env to get the password for client connections.
   370→- Watch journal for 60 seconds to confirm no crash-loop restarts
   371→- Verify guardian timer is running and has fired at least once
   372→- **Protocol verification**: If rocksdb:// was configured, verify SurrealDB started without errors. If it fails, revert to file:// as documented in Task 3.
   373→
   374→**Definition of Done:**
   375→- [ ] cohezion-surreal.service is active and running (not masked)
   376→- [ ] SurrealDB responds to queries
   377→- [ ] ngrok-tunnel.service is active (or stopped cleanly if no tunnel needed)
   378→- [ ] cohezion-guardian.timer is active and waiting
   379→- [ ] No services in crash loop (journal clean for 60 seconds)
   380→- [ ] cohezion-lab.service is disabled (documented: needs lab_driver.py)
   381→
   382→**Verify:**
   383→- `systemctl is-active cohezion-surreal.service` shows `active`
   384→- `journalctl -u cohezion-surreal.service --since "1 minute ago" --no-pager` shows healthy startup, no errors
   385→- `systemctl --user is-active cohezion-guardian.timer` shows `active`
   386→- `systemctl --user list-units cohezion* ngrok* --all --no-pager` shows no "failed" or "activating" units
   387→
   388→## Testing Strategy
   389→
   390→- **Task 1:** Manual SurrealDB start test validates DB recovery. If DB is unrecoverable, fresh init.
   391→- **Task 2:** Manual verification (file cleanup, journal vacuum)
   392→- **Task 3-6:** `systemctl show` to verify service properties match expected values. Confirm StartLimit in [Unit] section via `systemctl cat`.
   393→- **Task 7:** Syntax check (`bash -n`), dry-run execution, verify timer activation
   394→- **Task 8:** End-to-end: start services → watch journal → verify health → confirm guardian detects nothing wrong
   395→
   396→## Risks and Mitigations
   397→
   398→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   399→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   400→| MANIFEST-190909 is also corrupt or references missing SST files | Medium | High | Validate DB immediately in Task 1 (not Task 8). If validation fails, reinitialize with fresh DB — dev data is expendable |
   401→| rocksdb:// protocol behaves differently from file:// | Low | Medium | Test separately: first verify file:// works, then switch to rocksdb://. Two verification points, not one combined change |
   402→| MemoryMax=16G triggers OOM-kill of SurrealDB (66GB block cache) | Medium | Low | OOM-kill + StartLimitBurst=5 = at most 5 OOM kills in 10 min, then stop. Far better than infinite crash loop. SurrealDB 2.4.1 doesn't expose RocksDB block_cache_size tuning. Monitor empirically after startup |
   403→| Service guardian false-positives | Medium | Low | Guardian only STOPS user services, never restarts. For system services, alert only. False-positive = service stopped, user investigates. Safe default |
   404→| Guardian script hangs (systemd overloaded) | Low | Low | ExecStart wraps script with `/usr/bin/timeout 60`. TimeoutStartSec=90 in service unit. Prevents accumulation of blocked instances |
   405→| Accidental service re-enable during implementation | Low | Medium | Services masked in Task 1, unmasked only after hardening in Task 3/4. Prevents reboot from restarting unhardened services |
   406→
   407→## Open Questions
   408→
   409→- None — all questions resolved during investigation and plan verification
   410→
   411→### Deferred Ideas
   412→
   413→- Rewrite `DaemonManager` and `wake_up.py` to use systemd commands instead of custom process management
   414→- Research if SurrealDB has a config file or env var to set RocksDB block_cache_size explicitly (would eliminate the MemoryMax OOM risk)
   415→- Add Prometheus/Grafana monitoring for long-term observability
   416→- Create a `/wake` skill that integrates with the guardian for AI-tool-aware service management
   417→- Move cohezion-surreal.service from system scope to user scope (it already runs as mike-anderson, no need for system service)
   418→- Add polkit rule to allow user to stop system services without sudo (alternative to scope migration)
   419→

**Step 1.8: Get User Approval**

Here's a summary of the plan:

**Goal:** Fix the SurrealDB crash loop that took down your Framework Desktop, harden all services, and deploy a guardian safety net.

**Key findings:**
- SurrealDB RocksDB MANIFEST corruption has been crash-looping **129K+ times** since Feb 14 (4 days)
- `cohezion-surreal.service` had NO restart limits — systemd restarted it every 5 seconds forever
- Journal bloat hit 1.2GB → journald crashed → desktop portal crashed → 3 rapid reboots
- `cohezion-lab.service` has a User= bug causing "supplementary groups" error (also crash-looping)
- `SURREAL_PASS` in `.env` uses shell substitution that systemd reads literally (broken auth)
- 3 broken symlinks for deleted services (antiquarian, mycelium, shadow)

**8 tasks:**
1. Stop crash loops, mask services, fix MANIFEST, validate DB immediately
2. Clean up 57K log files + vacuum 1.2GB journal
3. Harden `cohezion-surreal.service` (restart limits, resource caps, protocol update, fix password)
4. Fix `cohezion-lab.service` (remove User=, fix dependencies, add limits, disable)
5. Remove broken symlinks
6. Harden ngrok + simulation service templates
7. Create bash service guardian (systemd timer, no Python deps)
8. Bring everything online and verify

  "new_string": "### Task 8: Create Claude Code PreToolUse Safety Hooks\n\n**Objective:** Create PreToolUse hooks that prevent AI coding tools from accidentally breaking services or creating runaway processes. These hooks fire before any Bash command executes in Claude Code (and can be adapted for Antigravity/Gemini CLI/OpenCode via their hook mechanisms).\n\n**Dependencies:** None (can run in parallel with other tasks)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/hooks/guard-services.sh`\n- Modify: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/settings.local.json` (add new PreToolUse hook)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Hook fires on `PreToolUse[Bash]` — intercepts all shell commands before execution\n- Block patterns that could create runaway services:\n  - `systemctl.*Restart=always` without `StartLimitBurst` (prevents creating unprotected services)\n  - `while true` or `for ((...))` infinite loops without timeout\n  - `nohup` or `&` backgrounding of processes that could outlive the session\n- Warn on patterns that touch service files without proper safeguards\n- Hook reads `$TOOL_INPUT` (JSON with command field) from stdin\n- Exit 0 = allow, Exit 2 = block with message\n- **Cross-tool portability:** Document how to adapt for Antigravity (`.antigravity/hooks/`), Gemini CLI (hook config), and OpenCode\n- The existing `settings.local.json` already has hook infrastructure (`PreToolUse[Bash]` matcher for `warn-sensitive-commands.sh`, but that file doesn't exist). We should create the missing file AND add our new hook.\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] guard-services.sh hook exists and is executable\n- [ ] Hook blocks `Restart=always` without `StartLimitBurst` in service file writes\n- [ ] Hook warns on `while true` / infinite loop patterns\n- [ ] settings.local.json updated with new hook entry\n- [ ] Documentation comment in hook explaining cross-tool adaptation\n\n**Verify:**\n- `bash -n /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/hooks/guard-services.sh` — syntax check passes\n- Hook correctly blocks a test pattern (simulated dangerous command)\n\n### Task 9: Integrate with Healing System and Add Structured Logging\n\n**Objective:** Wire the guardian's findings into Cohezion's self-healing system so crash-loop detection feeds back into compound engineering knowledge. Add structured JSON logging for all guardian actions.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 7 (guardian must exist)\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/guardian_reporter.py` (lightweight reporter)\n- Modify: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` (add JSON log output)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n- Guardian (bash) writes JSON events to `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/data/guardian_events.jsonl`\n- Each event: `{\"timestamp\": \"...\", \"service\": \"...\", \"event\": \"crash_loop|high_restarts|disk_alert\", \"action\": \"stopped|alert\", \"restarts\": N, \"details\": \"...\"}`\n- `guardian_reporter.py` is a separate lightweight Python script that:\n  - Reads `guardian_events.jsonl`\n  - Registers events with the healing system (`cohezion.healing.HealthStatus`)\n  - Can be run periodically or on-demand\n  - This keeps the guardian itself dependency-free (pure bash) while still feeding the compound loop\n- Add structured logging to guardian: each check writes a JSON line even when healthy (for dashboarding)\n- Token efficiency: events are JSONL (one line per event, easily grep-able, no full JSON parse needed)\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n- [ ] Guardian writes JSON events to guardian_events.jsonl\n- [ ] guardian_reporter.py reads events and logs to healing system\n- [ ] Both healthy and alert states are logged\n- [ ] Events are parseable with standard tools (`jq`, `grep`)\n\n**Verify:**\n- `bash /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/service_guardian.sh` produces JSONL output\n- `jq . /home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/data/guardian_events.jsonl` parses successfully\n- `uv run python scripts/guardian_reporter.py` runs without errors\n\n### Task 10: Bring Services Online and Verify\n\n**Objective:** Start all services, verify SurrealDB is healthy, confirm no crash loops, and run a smoke test.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 1, Task 2, Task 3, Task 4, Task 5, Task 6, Task 7, Task 8, Task 9"
